Button/Tooltip/Barracks=Gehenna Prime is a rugged and untamed world in the Gehenna system, renowned as the only terraformed and colonized planetoid of the seventeen in the system. Unlike many other worlds of the Imperium, Gehenna Prime remains a vast, unspoiled wilderness, with sprawling plains, towering mountains, and an ever-present sense of the untamed wild. The planet's capital and largest Hive city, Kravaster, serves as the beating heart of its human population.<n/><n/>Slightly larger than Terra, Gehenna Prime's faster rotation results in heightened volcanic activity. The Graildark Mountains, a massive belt of volcanoes encircling the equator, features both active and semi-active volcanoes, adding an ever-present threat to the world's stability. To the north and south of this fiery ring, the landscape transitions into jungle highlands, chains of extinct volcanoes, and eventually broad plains that stretch far beyond the cities.<n/><n/>The Hives of Gehenna Prime are scattered across these plains, with Kravaster standing as the largest and most prominent. These massive urban sprawl are surrounded by carefully cultivated croplands, shielded by heavy armourglass and plasteel to protect against the harsh environment and volcanic activity.

Button/Tooltip/Urun=Supreme Admiral Kor'O'Vanan is a highly respected commander of the T'au Navy, renowned for his tactical brilliance and unwavering determination. He led the fleet that confronted the Hive Fleet Gorgon during the devastating conflict at Kel'shan, a battle that would define his career. Kor'O'Vanan's leadership was pivotal during the initial invasion of the Gorgon at the colony world of Sha'draig, where, after assessing the dire situation, he ordered a strategic withdrawal to preserve the T'au forces. Despite the loss of the planet, Shas'el Vorcah's heroic sacrifice with a series of suicide missions eventually drove the Tyranids off, though the world was left decimated.<n/><n/>Kor'O'Vanan later commanded the T'au fleet at the Battle of Sol'ai Rift in 550.902.M41, as the Gorgon made its retreat from the Ka'mais System. Early in the engagement, the T'au appeared to have the upper hand, but the arrival of Ramsmiter and Razorfiend bio-ships, with their near-immunity to T'au Ion weapons, forced a retreat. Although the battle was lost, Kor'O'Vanan's fleet succeeded in destroying a number of the Gorgon's Narvhals, buying crucial time for the Kel'shan Sept to reinforce its defenses.<n/><n/>The final confrontation with Hive Fleet Gorgon took place at Kel'shan, where Kor'O'Vanan's fleet emerged victorious. However, three Hive Ships survived the battle and fled toward the galaxy's edge. Relentless in his pursuit, Kor'O'Vanan tracked the Tyranid survivors to Delmarra, where he engaged and destroyed one Hive Ship. Yet, the victory came at a cost--the Custodian-class Battleship Firestar was drawn into the asteroid belt and irreparably damaged. Despite this setback, Kor'O'Vanan pressed on, believing the surviving Hive Ships needed to make contact with other Tyranid forces to share vital intelligence, and continued his pursuit, ever determined to eliminate the Gorgon threat completely.

Param/Value/26232BAA=The main key to playing are three types of units and structures within the map that can be grouped together into three categories Spawners, Heroes, Arbiters.<n/><n/>Spawners<n/><n/>    Role: The spawners are the backbone of the army, producing basic units (minions, soldiers, etc.) that can flood the battlefield.<n/>    Mechanics:<n/>        Unit spawning: Regularly creates basic units, providing a steady flow of disposable forces.<n/>        Gassing: Spawners can be upgraded with gas that improves the quality of units and the amount.<n/>        Gas recovery: After upgrading, the gas resource returns the resource to the player<n/><n/>Heroes<n/><n/>    Role: Heroes are the primary damage-dealers and play a critical part in key battles.<n/>    Mechanics:<n/>        Permadeath: Heroes do not respawn.<n/>        Beacon activation: Heroes can interact with specific map objectives (beacons), but only under certain conditions usually containing mineral costs and specific heroes.<n/>        High impact: As they are key units, heroes have special abilities that can turn the tide of battles.<n/><n/>Arbiters<n/><n/>    Role: Arbiters focus on utility, mainly for teleportation.<n/>    Mechanics:<n/>        Teleportation: The ability to move units around the map, giving an advantage in terms of positioning, retreat, or surprise attacks.<n/>        No respawn: Like the heroes, arbiters do not respawn.
